### Page 1

Taiwan Information Technology

Investment case: We initiate coverage of Hon Precision, the global leader 
in test handlers and active thermal control (ATC) systems, with a Buy (1) 
rating. According to management, the company commands >70% market 
share in AI/HPC final test (FT) and c.50% in system-level test (SLT) by 
revenue, distinctly positioning it as one of the key global beneficiaries of the 
rising silicon complexity.

Multi-layer content stacking supports ASP and margin growth. As test 
durations continue to lengthen and thermal requirements rise, customers 
are required to deploy greater parallel testing capacity and higher-
specification handlers, creating a structural demand tailwind for both FT 
and SLT handler. We forecast rising AI chip power consumption and 
package complexity over our forecast horizon to drive adoption of higher-
value ATC systems, mega-tray handlers (adding 25-30% to ASP relative to 
standard dual-temp ATC systems), and AOI integration (adding a further 
10-15% to system ASP), supporting a multi-year increase in content per 
tool and expanding its gross margin from 56.8% to 59.2% over 2026-28E.

Recurring consumables and CPO testing expand long-term growth 
visibility. Cold plates generate high-margin, annuity-like revenue with 
every new chip platform which should benefit from shortening AI processor 
refresh cycles. Beyond consumables, Hon Precision is extending its testing 
platform into co-packaged optics (CPO), where optical-electrical co-testing 
could unlock a new addressable market beyond traditional GPU and ASIC 
testing. While near-term contribution remains limited, we view CPO as a 
strategic growth option that enhances the company’s long-term earnings 
visibility and valuation premium over our forecast horizon.

Catalysts: 1) capacity expansion ramp in 2027; 2) mega-tray penetration 
and higher-power ATC adoption; 3) initial CPO test solution qualification.

Valuation: We initiate with a Buy (1) rating and 12M TP of TWD6,365, 
based on a target PER of 30x applied on our 2027E EPS. The stock 
currently trades at c.19x forward PER, despite our projected 2026-28E EPS 
CAGR of 55%, implying a PEG of only 0.35x. We believe its current 
valuation heavily discounts macro noise with share price pulling back by 
c.53% from its historical high and derating from a cycle-peak multiple of 45-
50x 1-year-forward PER. Backed by solid order visibility through late-2027, 
we highlight Hon Precision’s risk-reward profile as decisively asymmetric.

Risks: 1) Slower-than-expected AI/HPC investment; 2) delayed ramp-up of 
new products; and 3) potential loss of market share.

Growth outlook Valuation Earnings revisions

Growth outlook

Hon Precision: revenue and gross margin outlook 
We expect Hon Precision’s growth momentum over 2026-
28E to be driven by increasing testing content per chip, 
supported by rising power consumption in AI/HPC chips, 
larger package sizes, and growing system complexity. We 
expect the transition towards high-end ATC handlers, 
mega-tray solutions, and integrated AOI functions to drive 
meaningful ASP expansion over our forecast horizon, with 
recurring cold plate demand providing an additional gross-
margin growth driver.

Source: Company, Daiwa research

Valuation   Hon Precision: 1-year-forward PER bands 
Our 12-month TP of TWD6,365 is based on a target PER 
of 30x applied to our 2027E EPS forecast. Following the 
strong share-price rally in 1H26, investor focus shifted 
away from Hon Precision due to limited near-term catalysts 
ahead of the 2H27 ramp-up of mega-tray handlers, CPO 
testing solutions and new capacity. We believe the recent 
53% pullback from its historical high on 29 May 2026 and 
derating from 45-50x 1-year-forward PER to c.19x more 
than prices in the macro uncertainties. At the current levels, 
the stock offers an attractive risk-reward profile, trading at 
a PEG of just 0.35x based on our 2026-28E EPS CAGR of 
55%.

Source: Bloomberg

Earnings revisions

Hon Precision: Bloomberg-consensus earnings revisions 
The Bloomberg consensus raised its 2026E and 2027E 
EPS estimates by 68% and 122%, respectively, over the 
past year, driven by stronger-than-expected AI-related 
demand and improving visibility on the company’s long-
term growth trajectory. We believe the upward revisions 
reflect rising confidence in volume and ASP expansion 
across the company’s key testing solutions, supported by 
accelerating AI infrastructure investments and expanding 
customer adoption.

Company profile 
Founded in 1999, Hon Precision is the global leader in semiconductor test handlers and active thermal 
control (ATC) systems. The company dominates the global AI/HPC final test handler market, supported 
by its advanced thermal management technologies. With over 70% market share in AI/HPC test 
handling, Hon Precision benefits from growing demand for AI, HPC and custom ASIC applications. Its 
solutions are widely adopted by leading OSATs and foundries worldwide, making it a key enabler of 
advanced semiconductor testing.

### Page 6

6

Hon Precision (7769 TT): 10 November 2026 
Growth Catalysts 
Multi-factor dollar content stacking 
We believe the primary growth catalyst for Hon Precision over our forecast horizon stems 
from multi-layered dollar content stacking, where the rise in machine unit value should 
outpace volume growth. Next-gen AI accelerators have historically exhibited more than 50-
70% increase in thermal design power (TDP) per generation, with platforms like Nvidia 
Rubin and Rubin Ultra surging to 2.3kW and 3.6kW vs. Blackwell’s 1.2kW to 1.4kW. 
Managing these extreme thermal loads makes high-end active thermal control (ATC) a 
necessity, driving a structural step-up in ASPs from standard handlers at approximately 
TWD6m to dual-temperature ATC systems at TWD12-15m, and tri-temperature ATC 
systems at TWD15-18m.

Concurrently, as the package size of next-generation ASICs/GPUs increases from the 
current 120mm tray specification to around 150mm, Hon Precision is introducing mega-
tray large-package solutions in October 2026, which command a 25-30% ASP premium 
over mainstream GPU handlers. As the majority of next-generation ASIC/GPU is set to 
exceed the boundary of 120mm, we estimate shipment penetration for mega-tray solutions 
will likely reach 40% in 2027E. Integrated functions of Automated Optical Inspection (AOI) 
will contribute an additional 10-15% ASP uplift to unit value of handler. Furthermore, this 
content expansion extends into the flagship mobile AP market as smartphone processors 
transition to multi-die packaging. For example, Apple’s A20 Pro processor has already 
adopted wafer-level multi-chip module (WMCM) packaging in 2026, requiring high-end ATC 
systems capable of managing up to 3,000W thermal dissipation to resolve device self-
heating.

Hon Precision: ASP multipliers

Source: Daiwa research

High-margin cold plate consumables  
In addition to capital equipment set sales, Hon Precision’s cold plate consumables 
business serves as a crucial recurring revenue engine and a primary driver of gross margin 
expansion. Cold plates carry the highest gross margin across all company product lines. 
Driven by the compression of AI processor lifecycles to 1-1.5 years (down from 2-3 years 
historically), each test handler requires 5-10 distinct, custom-engineered cold plates 
tailored to specific die footprints, socket layouts, and thermal hot-spot geometries. To 
conduct parallel test and maintain the throughput of chip testing, test handler for system 
level test (SLT) requires even more cold plates, which can go up to 24 plates. As a result, 
cold plate tooling generates high-frequency annuity-like sales that recur with every new 
chip platform iteration, independent of major equipment replacements.

Hon Precision: cold plate revenue trend

Testing duration and volume multiples 
We believe the rapid rise in AI chip complexity is driving structural growth in semiconductor 
testing intensity, positioning Hon Precision as a key global beneficiary through its 
leadership in high-parallelism test handlers. Driven by the mainstream adoption of 
heterogeneous integration (such as TSMC’s CoWoS and Intel’s EMIB), verifying complex 
AI silicon has extended test durations by 2x to 10x vs. standard consumer chips (5 to 10 
seconds). Final test durations for AI GPUs have expanded from hundreds of seconds to 
over 10 minutes per chip. To maintain daily manufacturing throughput despite this 10x 
spike, chipmakers must scale up massive parallel testing architectures and procure 
significantly more handlers, cementing Hon Precision as the primary test handler partner 
for tier-one US AI GPU and CPU players.

Concurrently, escalating chip complexity, TDP, and die costs make field return (RMA) 
penalties far exceed testing expenses, forcing cloud service providers (CSPs) and 
automotive OEMs to mandate system-level testing (SLT). Because SLT executes over 
extended durations ranging from minutes to hours per chip, high-volume production 
mandates automated parallel SLT handlers equipped with high-capacity ATC. Holding 
c.50% market share in AI/HPC SLT handlers alongside Chroma, Hon Precision is 
expanding beyond GPUs into custom CSP ASICs (eg, Google TPU and AWS) and server 
CPUs. As SLT adoption broadens across non-GPU devices, Hon Precision leverages this 
multi-year runway to transition into a full-spectrum semiconductor testing platform.

Global: growing chip complexity – testing time by AI/HPC chip

Source: Daiwa research

CPO unlocks new TAM beyond GPUs 
Beyond conventional electrical testing, Co-Packaged Optics (CPO) represents a multi-year 
paradigm shift towards a multimodal optical-electrical testing platform. Hon Precision 
currently monetises initial CPO deployments by supplying Final Test (FT) handlers for pure 
electrical testing (E/E test) of switch ASICs and Optical Engines (OEs) on production 
platforms such as Nvidia’s Spectrum-X and Quantum-X.

### Page 8

Hon Precision (7769 TT): 10 November 2026

However, the strategic growth catalyst is anchored in next-generation Insertion 4 (package-
level optical and electrical co-testing, O/E co-test). Because an undetected defect in a 
single optical component would force manufacturers to scrap an entire high-value CPO 
module, early and rigorous package-level validation is imperative for yield protection. 
Collaborating directly with ATE leader Teradyne and optical instrument specialists (such as 
Keysight), Hon Precision is developing a specialised ATC handler capable of simultaneous 
optical and electrical validation, including Bit Error Rate (BER), optical loopback, crosstalk, 
and high-power thermal management testing for integrated ASIC and Optical Engine 
modules in a single touchdown.

Engaged with Nvidia CPO switch programmes and actively expanding to additional ASIC 
clients, Hon Precision targets engineering verification in late-2026 and initial shipments in 
2027, ahead of 2028 volume production. Although we expect revenue contribution will 
likely be relatively low (<5% in 2027-2028), it drives valuation and market expectation to 
serve larger total addressable market (TAM).

Market Analysis 
Global testing equipment market 
According to TechInsights, global testing equipment capex is projected to expand 
significantly from USD8.3bn in 2024 to USD26.1bn by 2027E, representing a 46.4% 
CAGR. Notably, while SoC test systems front-loaded strong growth in 2024-25, before 
tapering to 9.6% YoY in 2027E on TechInsights estimates, package handling equipment is 
forecasted to exhibit strong structural resilience and longevity. Based on TechInsights, 
market capex for package handling equipment will likely more than double from USD941m 
in 2024 to USD2.6bn by 2027E (40.9% CAGR), re-accelerating to 46% YoY growth in 
2027E from 34% YoY in 2025. This cadence reflects the phased deployment of test 
infrastructure – initial tester investments are followed by high-volume back-end handling 
installations as advanced packaging (eg, CoWoS, Chiplets) scales into mass production.

Global semi testing equipment: capex  Global semi testing equipment: handler and prober growth

Source: TechInsights, Daiwa research  Source: TechInsights, Daiwa research

Handler market players 
Hon Precision’s overall share in the global test handler TAM rose from 35% in 2024 to 57% 
in 2025, and we forecast this to reach 75% in 2026E and 79% by 2027E. Within the high-
value AI and HPC Final Test (FT) handler segment, Hon Precision maintains a dominant 
market share, exceeding 70% in both sales value and shipment volume, per management. 
In the rapidly expanding AI and HPC SLT handler segment, Hon Precision holds c.50% 
market share in terms of revenue, per management, alongside its primary peer, Chroma, 
establishing a duopoly in global high-end SLT testing.

Hon Precision: handler market share forecasts

Source: Daiwa research

Outside of AI and HPC logic testing, the broader test handler landscape comprises global 
and regional vendors, including Cohu (COHU US, NR), Advantest (6857 JP, JPY28,375, 
Outperform [2]; covered by Daiwa analyst Takeshi Fukuyama), and mainland Chinese 
suppliers such as Changchuan Technology (300604 CH, NR) and JHT Design (603061 
CH, NR).

### Page 10

Hon Precision (7769 TT): 10 November 2026

While global incumbents rely on brand prestige, standard architectures, and bundled 
offerings, Hon Precision excels in leading-edge AI/HPC testing through its ultra-high-power 
Active Thermal Control (ATC) and rapid engineering turnaround. As multi-kilowatt dynamic 
loads (1.5kW–3kW+) and extreme warpage on >120mm packages push standard handlers 
beyond their limits, Hon Precision’s hyper-customised ATC fluidics, high-force micro-
mechanics, and modular designs deliver superior millisecond thermal clamping and agile 
package adaptation. Furthermore, while giants like Advantest dominate the SoC ATE tester 
market (>55% share), their in-house handlers focus primarily on memory and standard 
logic. Consequently, tier-one chipmakers and OSATs standardly decouple tester and 
handler sourcing, thus pairing Advantest or Teradyne testers with Hon Precision’s high-
power ATC handlers. Located directly within Taiwan’s advanced packaging cluster, Hon 
Precision provides real-time, on-site co-validation and custom cold-plate engineering that 
preserve formidable entry barriers against bundled solutions.

Meanwhile, although Chinese competitors have expanded aggressively in domestic mature 
nodes and conventional Pick-and-Place handlers, Hon Precision commands a decisive 
generational lead in high-complexity advanced packaging applications. Chinese vendors 
remain largely confined to lower-wattage consumer, automotive, and industrial IC testing, 
lacking the proven patents, structural rigidity, and micro-level force balancing required to 
safely apply hundreds of kilograms of contact force onto fragile, multi-chip CoWoS 
packages without cracking dies or warping substrates. Moreover, because enterprise AI 
chips cost tens of thousands of dollars each, tier-1 global fabless leaders and North 
American cloud service providers (CSPs) maintain virtually zero tolerance for testing 
failure, establishing an entrenched validation moat for Hon Precision. Free from 
geopolitical export constraints, Hon Precision can seamlessly deploy across global OSAT 
networks in Taiwan, North America, and Southeast Asia.

Balance sheet and cash flow 
Driven by surging AI/HPC testing demand, Hon Precision is actively expanding its capacity 
across the new Desheng facility, localised production at the Changshu site, and 
debottlenecking of headquarter efficiency. The company targets 40-50% capacity growth in 
2026 and another 50% increase in 2027. The expansion of manufacturing space and 
production facilities requires an increase in capital expenditure, which we project Hon 
Precision will ramp up to approximately TWD2.8bn in 2026E, TWD3.8bn in 2027E, and 
TWD4.6bn in 2028E. The company’s operating cash inflows are more than sufficient to 
fully fund factory expansions alongside a c.70% dividend payout ratio.

Hon Precision continues to maintain an un-levered balance sheet with zero financial bank 
debt. Cash and cash equivalents exceeded TWD53bn in 2025; we project this figure to 
surpass TWD75bn by 2028E. Its robust balance sheet underpins peer-leading shareholder 
returns, with ROE to expand from 34.5% in 2025 to 59.6% by 2028E. This self-funding 
cash engine supports management’s targeted c.70% dividend payout ratio.

Hon Precision (7769 TT): 10 November 2026 
Valuation and recommendation 
Initiating coverage with a Buy (1) rating 
We initiate coverage of Hon Precision with a Buy (1) rating and 12-month TP of TWD6,365, 
based on a target PER of 30x applied to our 2027E EPS forecast. When the stock rallied 
above TWD8,000 in 1H26, its forward PER multiple stretched to a cycle peak of 45-50x. 
However, we expect key growth catalysts, including a 50% aggregate capacity expansion, 
alongside higher-ASP shipments of large-form-factor mega tray handlers and co-packaged 
optics (CPO) test solutions, to drive its top line, primarily in 2H27. With existing production 
lines operating at full utilisation, investors are concerned over a potential temporary 
deceleration in growth momentum through 2H26-1H27, ahead of the next potential major 
capacity inflection in 2H27.

However, as the calendar rolls into 2027, we believe market attention will swiftly refocus on 
the company’s multi-year structural thesis. Easing capacity bottlenecks, combined with 
robust ASIC-driven adoption of mega tray architectures and ramping CPO test platforms, 
should spark a powerful volume-and-ASP expansion cycle. The stock is currently trading at 
a 19x 1-year-forward PER vs. its earnings CAGR of 55% over 2026-28E, translating into a 
0.35x PEG ratio. We believe the valuation now heavily discounts the near-term pause in 
revenue and macro noises, presenting a compelling risk-reward profile for long-term 
investors.

Risks to our call 
1. Slower-than-expected AI/HPC investment: A slowdown in AI infrastructure spending 
or delays in GPU, ASIC and server CPU ramps could reduce demand for high-end 
FT/SLT handlers and thermal-control solutions, weakening order growth and revenue 
visibility. 
2. Slower-than-expected new product adoption: Customer qualification delays or 
slower adoption of mega-tray handlers, AOI inspection systems and CPO FT solutions 
could delay product ramps, resulting in revenue growth below our expectations. 
3. Potential share loss: Competitors’ advances in thermal control, customer qualification 
of alternative suppliers or delays in meeting new testing requirements could weaken 
Hon Precision’s position on next-generation platforms, affecting market share and 
pricing power.

### Page 15

Hon Precision (7769 TT): 10 November 2026 
Appendix 
Company introduction 
Hon Precision was founded in Taichung in 2015, building on the technology and industry 
experience of Hon Technology, which was established in 1999. The company develops 
semiconductor test handlers and thermal-control solutions for AI GPUs, AI ASICs, server 
CPUs, automotive chips and consumer electronics, supporting customers from new-
product introduction (NPI) through volume production.

Hon Precision has two main product categories:

• Semiconductor test handlers: Final-test (FT) and system-level-test (SLT) handlers 
automate chip loading, positioning and sorting. These platforms can incorporate active 
thermal control (ATC) systems to maintain chips at specified temperatures during 
testing. 
• Testing fixtures and modules: Mainly customised cold plates and socket layout kits 
(SLKs), which provide thermal management and mechanical interfaces for different chip 
packages. Customers may require replacement cold plates and kits as package designs 
change, even when retaining existing handlers.

Hon Precision’s order mix is significantly weighted towards AI/HPC/ASIC applications, 
which accounted for 78% of orders in 1Q-3Q26, up from 72% in 2025. These applications 
include AI computing, servers and networking. Automotive represented 9% of orders, 
followed by mobile AP and communications at 7%, consumer electronics at 4%, and 
memory and MEMS at 2%.

As for capacity, Hon Precision’s manufacturing footprint is primarily based in Taiwan, with 
Taichung serving as the group’s main R&D, engineering, quality management, and 
equipment production hub. In addition, the company operates a Suzhou facility focused on 
manufacturing cold plates and related kits, while Taichung Plant 4 is expected to provide 
incremental production capacity to support future growth.

Shareholder structure 
Hon Precision’s major shareholders include several investment and development 
companies. As of 17 April 2026, Hung Min Development was the largest shareholder with a 
17.37% stake, followed by Hongji Investment at 12.08% and Hong Yu Development at 
7.80%. Chairman Wen-Ta Hsieh also directly held 1.77%. International institutional 
shareholders included Norges Bank and GIC, with disclosed stakes of 1.30% and 0.97%, 
respectively. The company has a free float of 48%, allowing broad public-market 
participation and supporting trading liquidity.

### Page 17

Hon Precision (7769 TT): 10 November 2026 
Appendix 
What is handler? 
A semiconductor test handler automates the movement, positioning and temperature 
conditioning of packaged chips during testing. It places each device into a test socket, 
applies the required contact force and sorts the device according to the test results. The 
tester performs the electrical measurements, while the handler ensures consistent 
mechanical and thermal conditions.

The process begins with packaged chips loaded into the handler in trays. After temperature 
conditioning, the handler transfers each chip into a test socket connected to the tester 
through a load board. Once electrical contact is established, the tester applies signals and 
measures the chip’s response. For ATC-equipped systems, a customised cold plate works 
with the active thermal control system to maintain the required chip temperature 
throughout testing. The handler then unloads and sorts the chips based on their test 
results.

Semiconductor test handler: workflow and thermal control system

Source: Hon Precision, Daiwa research

Handlers are used in both final test (FT) and system-level test (SLT). FT handlers work 
with automated test equipment (ATE) to check electrical characteristics and functionality 
against specifications. SLT handlers place chips into test positions connected to system-
level platforms, typically comprising test boards, test PCs and power supplies, to evaluate 
operation under conditions resembling actual use.

### Page 18

18

Hon Precision (7769 TT): 10 November 2026 
Semiconductor back-end testing FT workflow

Source: Semiconductor engineering

Thermal control becomes increasingly important as AI/HPC chips consume more power 
and adopt larger, more complex packages. Different test patterns change power 
consumption and heat distribution, requiring dynamic heating or cooling to keep the chip 
within a specified temperature range. Precise handling and temperature control support 
repeatable measurements, while parallel testing and efficient chip movement help improve 
overall test throughput.

### Page 1

Taiwan Information Technology

What’s new: We review Hon Hai’s 3Q26 results, announced 12 November. 
We are impressed with its opex control, which led to solid operating margin 
expansion despite gross margin dilution from rising AI server contribution.

What’s the impact: 3Q26 results beat. Net income was TWD78bn (EPS: 
TWD5.53), 25% higher than our estimate (TWD62bn) and 10% higher than 
the Bloomberg consensus (TWD70bn). Revenue was TWD3.0tn (+19% 
QoQ; +46% YoY) with better-than-expected smartphone contribution. The 
gross margin came in at 6.02% (vs. 6.12% in 2Q26), a 0.1pp decline QoQ 
on a rising cloud segment mix. That said, the operating margin expanded 
0.07pp to 3.83%, driven by strong opex control and better leverage.

Key updates: Hon Hai reaffirms its positive view of its 2027 revenue 
momentum, targeting double-digit growth YoY (similar to our forecast). The 
company believes its cloud business will remain a key growth driver (54% 
of total revenue in 3Q26). Regarding total AI server racks, Hon Hai expects 
to see high-double-digit growth in 2027 (vs. our expectation of c.30% YoY 
growth from GPU server plus a rising ASIC contribution; however, as ASIC 
AI servers typically utilise consignment models, its AI server revenue 
growth will be slightly diluted). As for smartphones, considering Hon Hai’s 
key clients’ models command high ASPs, we believe the impact from rising 
memory costs will be relatively smaller, but we expect the PC segment to 
decline YoY due to the negative impact from rising component costs.

During the 3Q26 results call, AI server end demand from the US was the 
hottest question, especially after the US mid-term elections. We believe 
management’s view on hyperscalers’ and neocloud’s near-term demand 
remains positive, but the long-term impact must be closely monitored. Also, 
as investors are worried about the AI server capex slowdown from CSPs, 
management also indicated that there would be new end demand from 
sovereign and enterprise groups, which could act as a second growth 
wave. For 4Q26, the company is guiding for >15% revenue growth QoQ 
and YoY given the ICT peak season along with rising AI server revenue (up 
by 5-15% QoQ due to ongoing AI server shipment strength).

What we recommend: We raise our 2026-28E EPS by 10-12% to reflect 
higher ASP assumptions for AI servers. We reaffirm our Buy (1) rating but 
trim the 12M TP to TWD320, using a lower target PER of 13x (prior 16x; vs. 
its past-3-year range of 8-18x) on our 1-year forward EPS, given the overall 
weaker sentiment. Downside risk: low demand for AI servers.

How we differ: Our 2026-27E revenue are above the consensus by 2-3%, 
likely as we are more optimistic on Hon Hai’s AI server shipments.

Company profile 
Established in 1974, Hon Hai Precision Industry (Hon Hai) started out making plastic products, 
connector products and cable assemblies. It is now the largest electronics manufacturing service (EMS) 
provider globally in terms of design, manufacturing, global logistics and after-market service.

### Page 5

5

Hon Hai Precision Industry (2317 TT): 12 November 2026 
ESG analysis

Update Date: 12 Nov 2026 
Source: Daiwa, Company

ESG risks
Risks Management Analyst comments
G
Executive/board
quality 2
Hon Hai has incorporated 4 independent members into its 7-member board of directors in pursuit
of better governance. However, the company's Chairman also serves concurrently as President,
implying an absence of separation between board leadership and executive management.
Capital management 1 Hon Hai has declared >40% payout ratio for most of the years since 2014, and >50% in the past 5 years, pointing to a shareholder-friendly dividend policy.
Related party &
transaction 2
Revenue from related parties accounted for 1.6% of total revenue for 2025, while purchases
from related parties constituted 0.1% of cost of sales. We believe there is limited risk from
related-party transactions.
S Supply chain management 2 Hon Hai adheres to green design concepts and incorporates environmental factors and pollution prevention measures during the design and development stages. The company's green product design guidelines include 4 axes: "energy-saving, non-harmful, reduction and easily recyclable."
S Labor practices 2
Hon Hai recognises employees as the most important asset and continues to invest in its
workforce. In terms of safety, Hon Hai has upgraded its occupational safety and health
management system since 2019, and obtained the ISO45001 and CNS45001 certificates. The
company held online activities related to factory safety and health. Overall, we note that Hon Hai
has improved in terms of labour management in recent years. Hon Hai established a team to
implement data security management. Moreover, it plans to conduct data security training and
education regularly for all employees. Hon Hai aims to achieve zero data security incident in the
future.
S Product design & lifecycle management 2 Hon Hai adheres to green design concepts and incorporates environmental factors and pollution prevention measures during the design and development stages. The company's green product design guidelines include 4 axes: "energy-saving, non-harmful, reduction and easily recyclable."
Company specific Management Analyst comments
E GHG emissions 2
In November 2020, Hon Hai announced its commitment to achieving net zero greenhouse gas
emissions by 2050. Its Scope 1 and 2 GHG emissions decreased by 53.6% in 2025 compared
with 2020, exceeding its reduction target of 21%. That said, the company's actual energy-saving
rate in mainland China was 5.2% in 2025, successfully achieving its energy-saving goal of 4.2%.
Note: Management score represents a company's ability to manage/benefit from certain ESG topics. The scores range from 1 to 3, with 1 being the strongest.

### Page 1

M
Idea
Alchip Technologies Ltd  | Asia Pacific
European NDR: strong five-year
outlook in AI ASICs; OW
Alchip's strong growth potential is rooted in US CSPs' AI ASIC
replacement cycle. Even after the recent rally, our price target
still implies 46% upside.
Right after its  3Q26 earnings call, we took Alchip's CEO, Mr. Johnny Shen, and CFO,
Mr. Daniel Wang, to visit European investors for discussion of the company’s long-
term strategy and outlook. Here are key takeaways:

Entering another phase of fast growth in the coming 4-5 years, with good
profitability: Alchip is sticking with its back-end design service focus, which
dovetails with CSPs' strategy of COT (customer-owned tooling). The long-term
trend is for CSP and AI lab customers to own the architecture and front-end design,
while pursuing efficiency in back-end design and production service. That strategy
can maximize the tokens generated per dollar  of AI capex. Besides winning future

generations with a major customer (AWS, 2nm and 1.4nm), Alchip has secured a
second big customer and is very close to winning a project with another US CSP (we
think it should be Google –  TPU computing dies). We project US$10bn total revenue
in 2028 –  4x our US$2.4bn estimate for  2026. Management thinks US$20bn
revenue p.a. by the end of this decade is possible, if the balance sheet permits.
More discussion of the second customer: The company still cannot disclose the

name of the second customer given the NDA, but reiterated that it expects a US$1bn
revenue contribution in 2027. Alchip provides part of the design service for the end
customer, and the gross margin is reasonably good. Although we don’t rule out the
possibility of another design service provider in this ASIC project for IP or front-end
design, we maintain our view that the end customer is a US CSP that uses Alchip for
back-end design service. Our industry checks don't see a CoWoS booking for this

ASIC, so we think it could be related to AI networking.
Getting close to winning a third big customer: In our October 11 report, Solid 2027
and 2028 outlook with potential catalysts, we already indicated that Alchip is a
strong candidate for Google TPU computing die back-end design service.
Management continued to show high confidence in a new project win without
specifying customer name; it expects some NRE revenue to come by the end of
2027. We asked which customer (second or third) could make a bigger revenue

contribution 2-3 years down the road – management thinks the third customer may
show bigger revenue potential.
Alchip's value underpinned by major customer (AWS): This could be reflected in
higher allocation of Trainium3 in 2027 and better business terms for Trainium4
(profits to grow with the volume upside). Management also expects 1.4nm design
activities to kick off some time in 2027 –  the key to five-year growth visibility.
Please see the following section for investors' feedback and our views.

### Page 2

M
Idea
2
Stronger revenue   guidance for 2027 and 2028
European investors' feedback
1. Alchip was somewhat overlooked in the AI ASIC market over the past two years,
compared with MediaTek and GUC, which continued to win new projects. Winning
second and third big customers is considered a re-rating driver – it mitigates the
concentration in a single customer (AWS).
2.  Meanwhile, European investors generally agree that AI ASICs should gain share
against GPUs, given tighter AI capex budgets. We share the view expressed in

recent comments from Alchip and MediaTek management: certain AI ASICs that
companies help to design provide very competitive TCO (total cost of ownership).
3. It is hard to track ASIC supply chain data points in real time, but the ASIC story
and Alchip's industry position can justify a long-term investment. Changes in
short-term data points wouldn't stop European investors from investing in Alchip.
4.  On valuation, investors think Alchip's 2028e implied P/E of 11x based on our EPS

estimate is relatively attractive vs. other global ASIC design service peers.
Considering this feedback, we think Alchip stock should  continue to re-rate: The stock
has been volatile, rebounding 50% since November 6 after a 40% pullback over October
12 to November  6. (The TAIEX was  +10% and -20% over those periods.) Based on our
estimates, P/E is now 17x for 2027 and 11x for 2028, lower than its historical average of
28x one-year forward P/E since 2019.

Key catalysts could be:
• November 30 –  AWS re:Invent conference with Trainium4 announcement;
• Supply chain identification of the second big customer's name and order potential;
• Confirmation of Google computing die back-end design service;
• Bigger Trainium3 revenue ramp in 3Q27 vs. 2Q27.

MS Rating
▪ We continue to believe that AI ASICs will

outgrow AI GPUs in the coming years, and
Alchip is one of the few ASIC design service
providers that can deal with increasingly
complex designs (at 2nm) with good
production/design track records for large
leading edge chips.
▪ AWS's announcement of its 3nm
Trainium3 chip matches the timing of
Alchip's 3nm project win. The growing
number of use cases for Trainium (e.g.,
Apple's adoption) bodes well for Alchip's
revenue growth in 2026.
▪ We believe Alchip has strong potential to

win Trainium4.
▪ Our price target implies 26x our 2027e
EPS, which we ﬁnd undemanding vs. our
>100% revenue CAGR for 2025-28e.

NT$6,880.00
30x 2027e EPS
AI chipsets and HPC projects start to
proliferate, amid substantial end demand.

Alchip's leading-edge tape-out numbers
increase signiﬁcantly, with some high-proﬁle
project wins at 3nm or even 2nm. Alchip is
able to secure enough foundry capacity
(front-end wafers and back-end packaging)
and substrates for all of its leading-edge
turnkey production. Alchip gradually gains
market share within the design service
market, amid continued proliferation of
custom chip demand.
NT$5,888.00
26x 2027e EPS
AI chipsets and HPC projects start to

proliferate amid strong end demand,
resulting in steady growth in tape-outs and
volume. There might be a slowdown in
Alchip's Chinese customers' design activity
for leading-edge chips as a result of US
export controls; we believe this would be
largely offset by ASIC demand strength
from non-China regions.
NT$2,465.00
11x 2027e EPS
AI chipsets and HPC projects lack concrete
end demand, resulting in delayed tape-outs
and mass production. Alchip's leading-edge

project pipeline deteriorates, or some
customers turn directly to TSMC. AI GPU's
dominance in AI computing compresses the
market share gain potential for overall ASIC,
with customers starting to slow their
custom silicon developments - and therefore
ASIC procurement plans.

12-18 months.

Greater China Technology Semiconductors
Asia Pacific
Industry View Attractive
Our recent downgrades have generated investor pushback. We
acknowledge that demand remains strong, but believe exposure
should be reduced before cloud-capex deceleration becomes

visible.
Following Cloud Semis: Peak Growth, Less Upside  and Old Memory: Peak Pricing
Power?, we address the main investor questions below.
1. Are the cuts to Montage’s earnings forecasts justified?
We acknowledge that our revised 2027 RCD shipment assumptions may prove too
conservative. We would characterize the shipment and revenue revisions as
cautious, incorporating uncertainty around procurement timing, platform transitions
and supply-chain execution. These assumptions leave room for upside if customer

orders and deployment proceed more smoothly than we currently allow for.
Structural content growth remains supportive, and sustained order strength would
warrant revisiting the degree of conservatism in our estimates.
2. What justifies the multiple compression (across memory and cloud semis),
and where is the catalyst?
Our central consideration is to reduce exposure before the cloud-capex deceleration
becomes visible. Valuations can adjust as investors reassess the durability of

growth, even while earnings continue to rise. Peaking earnings-revision breadth and
weaker visibility into 2028 server production reduce our confidence in extrapolating
recent growth rates.
For old memory, the concern is also around the sustainability of pricing and returns
as shortages narrow. Limited fixed-price LTAs enabled rapid price increases during
the upcycle, but provide less protection when demand weakens. Our updated
supply-demand models point toward normalization into 2028, supporting lower

valuation multiples before peak profitability rolls over.
The potential catalysts are softer order momentum and slower price increases. Such
timing remains uncertain, but we believe waiting for these developments to become
fully visible risks missing the valuation adjustment. Faster deployment, renewed
earnings upgrades and sustained capex acceleration beyond 1Q27 would challenge
our cautious view. (Continued)
Morgan Stanley does and seeks to do business with

communications with a subject company, public appearances
and trading securities held by a research analyst account.
November 15, 2026 03:36 PM GMT

### Page 2

M
 Idea
2

3. Have we changed our server or CPU shipment forecasts? If demand remains strong, 
why act now?
We have made no such industry-wide revisions. The hyperscalers’ 3Q results confirmed 
strong current spending. However, following the US midterms, our US team shifted 
approximately 5GW of expected US datacenter capacity additions from 2028 into 2029, 
equivalent to around 20% of previously expected 2028 additions (see Buy the Drop: AI 
Investment Opportunities Post the US Midterms). Our US team's hyperscaler capex 
forecasts remained unchanged at US$1.6trn and US$1.8trn for 2027 and 2028. This 
creates greater uncertainty around the timing of equipment procurement and deployment 
even with spending intentions intact.
We believe the combination of the completed 3Q earnings season and greater post-
election execution uncertainty provides a reasonable point to incorporate more risk. 
Current demand can remain strong while the balance of potential earnings revisions and 
valuation outcomes becomes less attractive.
4. Why retain Overweight on CXMT and Nanya Tech?
We differentiate by product mix, technology migration and valuation. Niche memory has a 
smaller supply base, more volatile pricing and generally less direct exposure to AI 
demand. Its pricing can respond sharply to relatively modest changes in supply, customer 
inventories and consumer demand. These characteristics make recent scarcity-driven 
profitability more vulnerable as supply catches up.
CXMT and Nanya also face memory-cycle risk. However, CXMT has additional growth 
drivers from capacity expansion, localization and process tech migration. We also continue 
to see tight DDR5 supply, with CXMT management indicating that customers had reserved 
2027 capacity for relevant enterprise products. For Nanya, stronger process technology 
and a clearer DDR5 migration path differentiate it from other DDR4 suppliers in our 
coverage, with DDR5 already contributing approximately 10% of its revenue.
Aspeed and Montage have concentrated exposure to server deployment and the cloud 
investment cycle. Their content-growth opportunities remain intact, but their earnings 
trajectories and valuations are particularly sensitive to changes in deployment timing and 
expectations for cloud-spending growth. We therefore see a stronger case for reducing 
exposure ahead of visible deceleration.

STOCK RATINGS
Morgan Stanley uses a relative rating system using terms such as Overweight, Equal-weight, Not-Rated or Underweight (see definitions below). Morgan Stanley does not assign ratings of Buy,
Hold or Sell to the stocks we cover. Overweight, Equal-weight, Not-Rated and Underweight are not the equivalent of buy, hold and sell.  Investors should carefully read the definitions of all
ratings used in Morgan Stanley Research. In addition, since Morgan Stanley Research contains more complete information concerning the analyst's views, investors should carefully read Morgan

Stanley Research, in its entirety, and not infer the contents from the rating alone.  In any case, ratings (or research) should not be used or relied upon as investment advice.  An investor's decision
to buy or sell a stock should depend on individual circumstances (such as the investor's existing holdings) and other considerations.

Analyst Stock Ratings
Overweight (O). The stock's total return is expected to exceed the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next
12-18 months.
Equal-weight (E). The stock's total return is expected to be in line with the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over

the next 12-18 months.
Not-Rated (NR). Currently the analyst does not have adequate conviction about the stock's total return relative to the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage
universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next 12-18 months.
Underweight (U). The stock's total return is expected to be below the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next

12-18 months.
Unless otherwise specified, the time frame for price targets included in Morgan Stanley Research is 12 to 18 months.
Analyst Industry Views
Attractive (A): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be attractive vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated
below.
In-Line (I): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be in line with the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.

Cautious (C): The analyst views the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months with caution vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.
Benchmarks for each region are as follows: North America - S&P 500; Latin America - relevant MSCI country index or MSCI Latin America Index; Europe - MSCI Europe; Japan - TOPIX; Asia -
relevant MSCI country index or MSCI sub-regional index or MSCI AC Asia Pacific ex Japan Index.

融程電(3416)：國防軍工引領成長 
建議：維持買進

一、融程電為強固型電腦廠商，
近年國防軍工營收大幅提升 
 融程電成立於 1996 年，公司前三大股東為研華、醫揚、廣積。融程電為強固型
平板電腦和手持式裝置專業廠商，產品廣泛應用於航太、船舶、軍警、交通運輸、
車廠維修檢測、醫管、石化、和製程控制等利基型產業；歐洲為融程電最大市場，
賓士、保時捷、Bosch 等皆為融程電客戶，美洲市場方面，智慧倉儲解決方案廠
商 Honeywell、堆高機品牌 CROWN 以及智慧醫療 Medtronic 等亦是融程電客
戶。近年國防軍工營收大幅提升，自 2024 年的高個位數佔比提升至 2025 年的
近兩成水準，2026 年已突破兩成水準；截至 3Q26 Edge AI 佔比約 10%。

二、3Q26 毛利率低於預期，係
因出貨較多倉儲客戶產品 
 3Q26 營收 14.13 億元，截至 3Q26 產品組合：車檢 21%、倉儲物流 23%、醫
療保健 12%、其他 44%(其中超過 25%為國防軍工)；獲利方面，毛利率 36.36%，
低於預期，係因 3Q26 出貨較多低毛利率 Honeywell 倉儲物流產品，營益率
19.05%，單季稅後 EPS 2.63 元。

三、2027 年仍由國防軍工驅動主
要成長 
 3Q26 毛利率受 Honeywell 倉儲物流產品出貨較多影響，Honeywell 2026 全年
訂單金額預估 2~3 億元，2027 年 Honeywell 目標回到往年全年 6 億營收水準，
目前仍在與客戶洽談新訂單；4Q26 軍工航太及車用等出貨比重提升，預期毛利
率較 3Q26 回升；整體預期 2026 全年營收+30%YOY，其中出貨量成長 10%，
其餘 20%來自 ASP 提升(包含漲價與產品組合轉佳)。 
融程電目前 BB Ratio 1.1，訂單能見度約 6~8 個月，由於缺料狀況緩解，客戶
下單能見度恢復正常水準，預期 2027 年受惠軍工航太、無人載具及 Edge AI 需
求成長，產品組合持續優化將推升 ASP 及毛利率。產品組合方面，截至 3Q26
其他業務營收佔比 44%，2027 年佔比有望提升至 50%，主由國防軍工驅動，
2027 年國防軍工預期再成長 30~40%，甚至有望上看超過 50%成長，融程電具
全球領先海事船舶電腦技術，拓展至地面工作站及無人機整機，無人載具現有月

기아 (000270)

26.3Q Review: 수익성에 대한 입증이 필요  Company Brief

26.3Q Review: 매출액 31.6조원, 영업이익 1.8조원  
기아는 26.3Q 매출액 31.6조원 (+10.1% YoY, -4.4% QoQ), 영업이익 1.8조원 
(+24.3% YoY, -30.8% QoQ, +5.8% OPM) 등의 실적을 기록했다. 26.3Q 영업이익은 
시장 컨센서스 (2.36조원)를 22.9% 하회했다 [iM증권 추정치: 매출액 31.5조원, 
영업이익 2.3조원]. 주요 구성 요소별 내용은 아래와 같다. 
① P: 26.3Q 26.3Q 전동화 차량 판매는 30.0만대 (+47% YoY, 판매 비중 
36.2%)를 기록했는데, 이 중 HEV 17.2만대 (+46% YoY), EV 12.3만대 
(+75% YoY)로 구성됨. 미국 HEV 판매가 전년 동기 대비 118% 증가하며 
시장 성장률을 약 4배 상회했고, 특히 서유럽 EV 판매가 전년 대비 163% 
증가함. 단 26.3Q 중 원화가 빠르게 강세로 전환되며 원/달러, 원/유로 
평균 환율은 전분기 대비 각 5.2%, 6.2% 하락함에 따른 – 효과 관찰됨. 
② Q: 26.3Q 도매 판매는 84.7만대 (+7.9% YoY)를 기록했고, 북미, 유럽, 
인도 등 주요 시장에서 고른 성장 나타남. 26.3Q 소매 판매 또한 82.9만대 
(+6.9% YoY)로 글로벌 산업 수요 (-6% YoY)를 상회. 북미는 HEV, 내수와 
유럽은 EV, 인도는 신형 셀토스와 소넷을 중심으로 판매가 확대됨 (이하 
26.3Q 권역별 도매 판매 YoY, 국내: +2.2%, 북미: +7.6%. 유럽: +19.3%. 
인도: +38.5%). 이란 사태 장기화로 아중동 도매 판매는 전년 동기 대비 
28.4% 감소했으나, 수요가 견조한 중남미와 CIS로 물량을 전환하며 
감소분을 상당 부분 만회함. 연간 도매 판매 가이던스 (335만대)를 유지함. 
③ C: 26.3Q 관세 비용은 약 0.7조원으로 추정되며, 전년 동기 대비 
5,000억원 감소함 (25.2Q 0.7조원, 25.3Q 1.2조원, 25.4Q 1.0조원, 26.1Q 
0.8조원). 반면 인센티브 증가 및 가격 인하 7,270억원, 서유럽 EV 비중 
확대에 따른 믹스 악화 4,650억원, 원자재 가격 상승 영향 3,680억원 등 
총 1.6조원의 감익 요인이 발생함. 이에 26.3Q 매출원가율은 84.1%로 
전년 동기 대비 3.0%p 상승함 (인센티브 +1.9%p, 재료비 +1.2%p). 다만 
26.3Q 기말 환율 급락하며 판매보증충당부채 환입 규모가 축소되었고 
판매보증비 약 9,000억원 감소. 판매관리비율은 10.1%로 3.7%p 개선됨. 
26.3Q 기아 순이익은 1.29조원으로 전년 동기 대비 9.6% 감소했다. 원화 강세에 
따라 외환환산 관련 이익이 전년 동기 540억원 이익에서 3,718억원으로 손실 
전환됐고, 현대모비스 등 관계사 이익 감소로 지분법손익 또한 360억원 감소했기 
때문이다 (현대차 순이익 감소 논리와 동일). 1) 산업 수요 감소와 중동 판매 차질에도 
권역별 파워트레인 대응을 통해 그룹사 대비 가지고 있는 외형 성장성을 증명했으나, 
2) 유럽 EV 판매 확대 과정에서 발생한 인센티브와 믹스 악화라는 비용 부담, 원자재 
가격 상승이 관세 감소 효과를 대부분 반납했고, 3) 분기 중 급격한 원화 강세가 
더해지며 시장 기대를 크게 하회하는 수익성을 기록했다는 것이 26.3Q 실적 총평이다.

실적 발표 후 주가 급락: 성장에도 불구하고 수익성에 대한 우려  
26.10.30 실적 발표 당일 기아 주가는 11.7% 하락했다. 하루 전 발표된 그룹사 실적 
발표와 상반된 모습이었다. 시장이 이번 실적에서 확인한 우려는 아래와 같다. 첫째, 
성장과 이익의 괴리다. 관세 영향을 제외한 26.3Q 영업이익은 2.52조원으로 전년 
동기(2.70조원) 대비 오히려 1,770억원 감소했다. 판매 증가에 따른 물량 효과 
(+4,420억원)와 환율 효과 (+5,290억원)가 발생했음에도, 인센티브와 믹스 악화로 이를 
대부분 반납한 것이다. 기아의 외형 성장 상당 부분이 유럽 EV 판매 증가에서 나오는데, 
해당 시장에서는 중국 EV 업체와의 경쟁으로 공격적인 프로모션이 불가피하다. 26.3Q 
유럽 권역에서의 중소형 EV 판매가 사업 계획을 크게 웃돌며 EV 믹스는 13.1%에서 
14.8%로 확대됐다. 시장은 이를 M/S를 위해 수익성을 훼손하고 있다고 받아들이는 
것이다. 둘째, 당장 26.4Q에도 유의미한 수익성 개선이 없을 것이라는 점이다. 기아는 
26.3Q 컨퍼런스 콜에서 4Q 영업이익률이 3Q 수준과 크게 다르지 않을 것이며, 연초 
제시한 연간 가이던스 (영업이익 10.2조원, +8.3% OPM)에 다소 미달할 것이라고 
밝혔다. 중동 분쟁의 영향이 최소 연말까지 이어지고, 원자재와 물류비 부담 또한 
지속될 것이 회사의 판단이다. 무엇보다 원/달러 환율이 26.2Q 평균 약 1,505원에서 
26.3Q 약 1,430원, 현재 1,320~1,340원 수준까지 하락하며 이에 따른 - 영향이 커질 
수 있다. 셋째, 그룹사와의 이익 체력 비교다. 현대차는 26.3Q 파업과 생산 차질로 도매 
판매가 11.3% 감소했음에도 원가 절감을 통해 영업이익이 5.5% 증가했고, 시장 
기대를 상회했다. 반면 기아는 판매가 7.9% 증가했음에도 영업이익이 컨센서스를 20% 
이상 하회했다. 26.3Q 양사 영업이익 격차는 약 8,600억원까지 벌어진 상황이다.  
단기 노이즈를 제외한다면 기아의 기업 가치는 여전히 높다  
그럼에도 당사는 기아의 EV 전략, 낮은 수익성을 이유로 중장기 기업 가치에 대한 
기대를 낮출 이유는 없다고 판단한다. 첫째, 외부 요인을 걷어내면 본원적인 수익 
창출력은 유지되고 있다. 기아는 26.3Q 영업이익률을 낮춘 외부 요인을 원자재 1.2%p, 
중동 판매 차질 0.7%p, 환율 1.7%p 등 총 3.6%p로 분석했다. 즉 중동 분쟁이 
해소되면 상당 부분이 사라질 일회성 요인이다. 둘째, EV 수익성 개선 경로는 HEV가 
걸어온 길과 같다. 기아는 HEV 역시 도입 초기에는 수익을 내지 못했으나 현재는 ICE에 
버금가는 수익성을 내고 있다며, 26~27년 현재의 EV 전략을 유지하겠다고 밝혔다. 
유럽 ICE 시장은 26.3Q 전년 동기 대비 약 20% 축소된 반면 EV 시장은 50% 이상 
성장하고 있다. 전환기에 스케일을 확보하지 못하면 EV 마진을 개선할 방법도 없다. 
기아는 향후 3년간 배터리, 모터, 히트펌프 등 주요 부품에서 연 10% 수준의 원가 
절감을 추진하고, 2~3년 내 EV를 HEV와 같은 수익성 경로에 올려놓겠다는 계획이다. 
셋째, 중장기 성장 계획을 수정하지 않았다. 기아는 27년 사업 계획이 CID 당시 제시한 
숫자와 큰 차이가 없을 것이며, 중장기 영업이익률 10% 목표도 유지했다. IAA 시행에 
대비해 슬로바키아 공장에서 EV를 전량 생산하고 ICE 국내 수출 방안도 검토 중이다. 
주주환원 측면에서도 TSR 35%를 지키고, 자사주 매입보다 배당을 우선하겠다는 
입장이다. 10/30 종가와 최소 DPS 기준만으로도 배당수익률은 6.3%에 달하고 있다.

  26.4Q 기아 매출액 31.8조원, 영업이익 2.2조원 전망 
기아 26.4Q 실적을 매출액 31.8조원 (+13.2% YoY), 영업이익 2.2조원 (+20.8% YoY, 
+7.0% OPM)으로 전망한다. 26.4Q 판매 성장세는 이어지고, 3Q에 집중 반영된 원자재 
부담 일부 완화와 계절적 성수기 효과로 3Q 대비 수익성은 소폭 개선될 것이다. 다만 
1) 원화 강세에 따른 평균 환율 하락, 2) 유럽 EV 인센티브의 높은 수준 유지 (미국은 
할부 금리 상승으로 QoQ 약 200달러 증가, 유럽은 전분기와 유사), 3) 중동 판매 
차질과 원자재, 물류비 부담 지속을 감안하면 의미 있는 반등은 어렵다. 이는 4Q 
영업이익률이 3Q 수준과 크게 다르지 않을 것이라는 회사의 언급과도 궤를 같이한다. 
기아 목표주가 150,000원으로 하향  
기아 목표주가를 26.4Q 추정치 하향을 반영해 150,000원으로 하향한다. 기아의 판매 
경쟁력은 금번 분기에도 증명됐다. 다만 EV 판매 확대가 수익성 개선으로 연결된다는 
점이 숫자로 확인되기 전까지, 시장은 기아의 성장에 할인율을 적용할 것이다. 중동 
분쟁 해소와 EV 원가 절감이 맞물리는 27년이 기아가 수익성을 증명할 시점이 될 
것이며, 6%를 상회하는 배당수익률이 그때까지 주가의 하방을 지지할 것으로 판단된다.

Compliance notice 
당 보고서 공표일 기준으로 해당 기업과 관련하여, 
 회사는 해당 종목을 1%이상 보유하고 있지 않습니다.  
 금융투자분석사와 그 배우자는 해당 기업의 주식을 보유하고 있지 않습니다.  
 당 보고서는 기관투자가 및 제 3자에게 E-mail등을 통하여 사전에 배포된 사실이 없습니다.  
 회사는 6개월간 해당 기업의 유가증권 발행과 관련 주관사로 참여하지 않았습니다. 
 당 보고서에 게재된 내용들은 본인의 의견을 정확하게 반영하고 있으며, 외부의 부당한 압력이나 간섭 없이 작성되었음을 확인합니다. 
본 분석자료는 투자자의 증권투자를 돕기 위한 참고자료이며, 따라서, 본 자료에 의한 투자자의 투자결과에 대해 어떠한 목적의 증빙자료로도 사용될 수 없으며, 어떠한 경우에도 작성자 및 
당사의 허가 없이 전재, 복사 또는 대여될 수 없습니다. 무단전재 등으로 인한 분쟁발생시 법적 책임이 있음을 주지하시기 바랍니다.

[투자의견] 
종목추천 투자등급  
종목투자의견은 향후 12개월간 추천일 종가대비 해당종목의 예상 목표수익률을 의미함.  
 Buy(매수): 추천일 종가대비 +15% 이상  
 Hold(보유): 추천일 종가대비 -15% ~ 15% 내외 등락 
 Sell(매도): 추천일 종가대비 -15% 이상 
산업추천 투자등급

 Overweight(비중확대) 
 Neutral(중립) 
 Underweight(비중축소)

주가 추이

3분기 영업이익은 지상방산 매출 감소와 일회성 비용 등의 영향으로 
기대치를 대폭 하회했습니다. 4분기 실적 회복과 중장기 실적 성장 대
비 저평가된 밸류에이션에 주목합니다. 
3Q26 Review: 영업이익 0.5조원으로 컨센서스 대폭 하회 
3분기 매출액은 6.1조원으로 전년대비 6.0% 감소했다. 항공우주 부문
과 연결자회사들 성장에도 불구하고 지상방산 매출이 전년대비 약 0.9
조원 감소한 영향이다. 지상방산 내수와 수출 매출은 각각 0.7조원, 0.6
조원으로 전년대비 28.6%, 50.4% 감소했다. 내수는 대전 사업장 생산 
중단, 수출은 4분기 납기 집중에 기인한다. 항공우주 매출은 군수사업 
물량 증가로 전년대비 9.3% 증가한 0.7조원을 기록했다. 연결 자회사
인 한화오션과 한화시스템 매출은 각각 3.4조원, 0.8조원으로 전년대비 
12.9%, 30.1% 증가했다. 영업이익은 0.5조원으로 전년대비 49.2% 감
소했으며 영업이익률은 7.4%로 6.3%P 하락했다. 한화오션과 한화시스
템의 이익 증가에도 지상방산 이익 감소와 필리조선소 적자 반영이 영
향을 미쳤다. 지상방산은 일회성 비용 약 460억원, 원화 강세, 매출 감
소에 따른 고정비 부담 증가가 복합적으로 작용했다. 세전이익은 한화
오션의 외화환산손실 영향으로 -0.2조원을 기록하며 적자전환했다.  
실적 회복과 저평가 매력에 주목할 시점 
4분기 영업이익은 1.7조원으로 추정하며 시장 기대치를 상회할 전망이
다. 지상방산 수출 인도 물량이 집중되는 영향이다. 2027년 예상 실적 
기준 P/E는 12.5배로 경쟁사 Rheinmetall(22.5배) 대비 낮다. 약 35조
원의 방산 수주잔고를 기반으로 최소 2029년까지 실적 성장이 예상되
는 만큼 저평가 매력이 높다. 신규 수주 시점과 실적에 대한 우려가 상
존하지만 현재 밸류에이션에는 상당 부분 반영된 것으로 판단한다. 미
국 중간선거 등 국방예산 관련 정책 불확실성과 환율 부담이 완화되면 
투자자들의 관심은 4분기 실적과 2027년 신규 수주로 이동할 전망이
다. 연말 주요국의 국방예산 확정은 신규 수주 시점에 대한 불확실성을 
낮추는 계기가 될 수 있다.  
투자의견 BUY, 목표주가 1,300,000원 유지 
글로벌 재무장 수요 확대에 따른 동사의 중장기 성장 전망은 여전히 
유효하다. 투자의견 BUY와 목표주가 1,300,000원을 유지한다.

[ Compliance Notice ] (공표일: 2026 년 10 월 30 일) 
이 자료는 조사분석 담당자가 객관적 사실에 근거해 작성하였으며, 타인의 부당한 압력이나 간섭없이 본인의 의견을 정확하게 
반영했습니다. 본인은 이 자료에서 다룬 종목과 관련해 공표일 현재 관련 법규상 알려야 할 재산적 이해관계가 없습니다. 본인은 이 
자료를 기관투자자 또는 제 3 자에게 사전에 제공한 사실이 없습니다. (채운샘) 
저희 회사는 공표일 현재 이 자료에서 다룬 종목의 발행주식을 1% 이상 보유하고 있지 않습니다. 
한화에어로스페이스는 당사와 ‘독점규제 및 공정거래에 관한 법률’ 에 따른 계열회사 관계에 있는 법인입니다. 
이 자료는 투자자의 증권투자를 돕기 위해 당사 고객에 한하여 배포되는 자료로서 저작권이 당사에 있으며 불법 복제 및 배포를 금
합니다. 이 자료에 수록된 내용은 당사 리서치센터가 신뢰할 만한 자료나 정보출처로부터 얻은 것이지만, 당사는 그 정확성이나 완
전성을 보장할 수 없습니다. 따라서 이 자료는 어떠한 경우에도 고객의 증권투자 결과와 관련된 법적 책임소재에 대한 증빙으로 사
용될 수 없습니다.

### Page 6

한화에어로스페이스 (012450) [한화리서치]

6 
[ 종목 투자등급 ] 
당사는 개별 종목에 대해 향후 1 년간 +15% 이상의 절대수익률이 기대되는 종목에 대해 Buy(매수) 의견을 제시합니다. 또한 절대수
익률 -15~+15%가 예상되는 종목에 대해 Hold(보유) 의견을, -15% 이하가 예상되는 종목에 대해 Sell(매도) 의견을 제시합니다. 밸류
에이션 방법 등 절대수익률 산정은 개별 종목을 커버하는 애널리스트의 추정에 따르며, 목표주가 산정이나 투자의견 변경 주기는 종
목별로 다릅니다.

[ 산업 투자의견 ] 
당사는 산업에 대해 향후 1 년간 해당 업종의 수익률이 과거 수익률에 비해 양호한 흐름을 보일 것으로 예상되는 경우에 Positive(긍정
적) 의견을 제시하고 있습니다. 또한 향후 1 년간 수익률이 과거 수익률과 유사한 흐름을 보일 것으로 예상되는 경우에 Neutral(중립적) 
의견을, 과거 수익률보다 부진한 흐름을 보일 것으로 예상되는 경우에 Negative(부정적) 의견을 제시하고 있습니다. 산업별 수익률 전
망은 해당 산업 내 분석대상 종목들에 대한 담당 애널리스트의 분석과 판단에 따릅니다.

[ 당사 조사분석자료의 투자등급 부여 비중 ] (기준일: 2026 년 9 월 30 일)

### Page 1

HD 현대중공업 (329180/KS) 
실적&임단협 우려 해소, 발전엔진 수요 폭발적 
2026-10-29

조선/방산. 한승한, R.A 고서영 / shane.han@sks.co.kr / 3773-9992

매수(유지) 
목표주가: 640,000 원(하향) 
현재주가: 285,500 원 
상승여력: 124.2%

Signal: 3Q26, 조업일수 감소 및 환율 하락으로 시장 예상치 하회 
Key: 4Q26~1Q27 부터 본격적인 발전엔진 및 FDC 수주 모멘텀 전망 
Step: 상선 우려 감안해도 엔진&FDC 으로 밸류 매력도 충분. 매수 기회

3Q26 Review: 시장예상치 하회하는 실적 시현  
3Q26 연결 매출액 5조 7,536억원(+30.2% YoY, -9.1% QoQ), 영업이익 8,992억원
(+61.3% YoY, -13.5% QoQ)으로, 시장 예상치(9,792억원)를 하회했다. 조업일수 감소
(휴가 10 일+중대재해 4일) 및 환율 하락 영향(450 억원)과 더불어, 임단협 관련 예상 비
용 중 75%를 이번 분기 선반영 했기 때문이다. 상선 수주 빈티지는 중형 선박 기준 ‘23 년 
9.6%, ‘24년 65%, ‘25년 25%, 대형 선박 기준 ‘22년 4%, ‘23년 45%, ‘24년 31%, 
‘25년 20%로, 선가 상승효과 지속되는 중이다. 특히 해양 부문에서 루야 프로젝트의 공정
률 확대 효과 및 C/O로 180억원의 환입 발생으로 전 분기 대비 큰 폭의 이익 성장 시현했
다. 최근 키윗과 미국 내 합작 조선소 설립을 추진하며, 미 군수지원함 초도 2척 수주 및 향
후 물량 건조, 그리고 HII와의 함정 선체블록 하도급 사업 수주까지 기대할 수 있다.

투자의견 ‘매수’ 유지, 목표주가 64 만원으로 하향 
HD 현대중공업에 대한 투자의견 ‘매수’를 유지하고, 목표주가는 기존 82 만원에서 64 만원
으로 하향한다. 최근 상선 실적 피크아웃 우려에 따른 조선주 주가 급락으로 멀티플 하향 조
정했다. 하지만 상선 부문의 우려를 감안하더라도 상선 사이클과 무관한 육상 발전 엔진 및 
FDC 에 대한 글로벌 하이퍼스케일러들의 수요 확대는 지속되고 있는 중이다. 올해 공시 기
준으로 육상발전 엔진 약 1.6GW 수주한 것으로 알려졌으나, 미공시된 약 1.4GW 까지 포
함 시 총 3.0GW 이상을 수주한 상황이다. 캐파 증설 기준으로 ‘28 년 1.5GW, ‘29 년 
3GW 캐파에 해당하는 물량을 이미 대부분 채웠으며, 기존 계획 대비 빠른 준공 혹은 공정
률 확대, 더 나아가 추가적인 캐파 증설까지 고려할 수준으로 파악된다. 빠르면 올해 연말 
혹은 내년 초부터 4 행정 중속엔진&FDC 본격 수주를 통해 중장기적 신성장 동력 확보할 
전망이다. 현 주가 레벨에서 상승여력 충분히 남아있다는 판단으로, 3Q 실적 및 임단협 불
확성 해소된 현 시점에서 적극적 매수 의견 및 조선주 최선호주(Top-Pick)로 제시한다.

HD현대중공업 수정TP(원)

Document 268: [373220] LG에너지솔루션(373220) 보상금 제외해도 호실적LG에너지솔루션(373220) 보상금 제외해도 호실적보상금 제외해도 호실적 - 기업

Source PDF: all report pdf/[373220] LG에너지솔루션(373220) 보상금 제외해도 호실적LG에너지솔루션(373220) 보상금 제외해도 호실적보상금 제외해도 호실적 - 기업.pdf
Report source: Hankyung
Date: 2026-10-12
Pages: 10
Status: ok

### Page 1

(373220) 
LG 에너지솔루션 
2026.10.12

에너지/인프라/배터리 황성현, Ph.D. 
6878 / tjdgus2009@eugenefn.com

보상금 제외해도 호실적 
 투자의견 BUY(유지) 
 목표주가 530,000 원(상향) 
 현재주가 401,000 원(10/8)

 [투자의견] 당사는 LG에너지솔루션에 대해 실적 추정치 상향을 반영하여 목
표주가를 530,000원으로 13% 상향하고, 투자의견 BUY를 유지한다. 
3Q26부터 ESS 물량 증가에 따른 가동률 상승 효과가 본격적으로 실적에 
반영되기 시작했으며, 4Q26에도 AMPC 제외 흑자 기조를 이어갈 전망이
다. 전사 가동률은 3분기 65%까지 상승한 것으로 파악되며, 이에 따른 펀
더멘털 개선이 본격화되고 있다고 판단한다. 
 [3Q26 잠정 실적 Review] 매출액 9.6조원(+28%qoq, +59%yoy), 영업이
익 7,560억원(+567%qoq, +26%yoy)을 기록하며 당사 추정을 큰 폭으로 
상회했다. 북미 생산보조금(AMPC)을 제외한 매출액은 9.2조원, 영업이익은 
3,391억원(영업이익률 3.7%)으로, 2분기(-1,277억원, -1.7%) 대비 흑자 
전환에 성공했다. ① 원통형은 테슬라향 물량 호조에 힘입어 영업이익 
2,010억원(영업이익률 8.1%)을 달성한 것으로 추정된다. ② EV는 유럽 폴
란드 공장의 가동률 상승과 GM 얼티엄의 8월 가동 개시로 매출이 증가했
다. 여기에 완성차 업체 보상금 3,000억원이 반영되며 영업이익 1,090억
원(영업이익률 2.5%)을 기록한 것으로 추정된다. ③ ESS는 매출액이 50% 
이상 급증하며 실적 서프라이즈를 견인했다. AMPC 포함 영업이익은 4,460
억원(영업이익률 16.0%)으로 추정된다. 2분기 매출 성장률 +17%qoq에 
이어 3분기 성장세가 더욱 가팔라졌다는 점이 긍정적이다. 
 [4Q26 Preview] 매출액 8.0조원(-17%qoq, +30%yoy), 영업이익 6,790
억원(-10%qoq, 흑전yoy)으로 추정한다. 4분기 실적은 ESS 수익성 개선 
속도와 완성차 업체 보상금 반영 규모에 따라 변동할 전망이다. 동사는 기존 
가이던스를 통해 하반기 북미 ESS 생산량이 상반기 대비 2배 증가할 것으
로 제시한 바 있다. 3분기 ESS는 AMPC를 제외하더라도 양호한 수익성을 
기록한 것으로 추정된다. 향후 팩 생산 병목 해소와 북미 공장 가동 안정화
에 따른 고정비 부담 완화가 이어지며 추가적인 손익 개선이 가능할 전망이
다. EV 역시 GM 얼티엄 생산 확대와 유럽 공장 가동률 상승이 지속되며 
AMPC 제외 적자 규모를 점진적으로 축소할 것으로 예상한다. 
 [투자포인트] 9월 미시간 랜싱 공장의 개소식이 개최됐다. 랜싱 공장은 당초 
EV용 파우치 배터리 생산거점으로 계획됐으나 ESS 중심으로 역할이 재편
됐으며, 내년에는 10GWh 이상의 각형 LFP 생산라인이 추가될 예정이다. 
2027년에는 신규 대규모 증설보다 기존 북미 생산거점의 가동률 상승과 
ESS 생산량 확대를 통한 수익성 개선이 핵심이 될 전망이다. 북미 전력망 
접속 지연과 AI 데이터센터의 부하 변동성 확대에 따라 Stand-alone ESS, 
BTM, UPS, BBU 수요가 증가하면서 신규 수주도 지속 확대될 것으로 예상
한다. 특히 동사의 Non-PFE 요건 충족과 SI· O&M을 아우르는 End-to-
End 솔루션 역량이 북미 ESS 시장에서 경쟁력으로 작용할 전망이다.

Compliance Notice 
당사는 자료 작성일 기준으로 지난 3 개월 간 해당종목에 대해서 유가증권 발행에 참여한 적이 없습니다 
당사는 본 자료 발간일을 기준으로 해당종목의 주식을 1% 이상 보유하고 있지 않습니다  
당사는 동 자료를 기관투자가 또는 제 3 자에게 사전 제공한 사실이 없습니다 
조사분석담당자는 자료작성일 현재 동 종목과 관련하여 재산적 이해관계가 없습니다 
동 자료에 게재된 내용들은 조사분석담당자 본인의 의견을 정확하게 반영하고 있으며, 외부의 부당한 압력이나 간섭 없이 작성되었음을 확인합니다 
동 자료는 당사의 제작물로서 모든 저작권은 당사에게 있습니다 
동 자료는 당사의 동의 없이 어떠한 경우에도 어떠한 형태로든 복제, 배포, 전송, 변형, 대여할 수 없습니다 
동 자료에 수록된 내용은 당사 리서치센터가 신뢰할 만한 자료 및 정보로부터 얻어진 것이나, 당사는 그 정확성이나 완전성을 보장할 수 없습니다. 따라서 어떠
한 경우에 동 자료는 고객의 주식투자의 결과에 대한 법적 책임소재에 대한 증빙자료로 사용될 수 없습니다

Document 344: France_ double-digit growth in business creations, a slowdown in the rise in bankruptcies and stable non-performing loans ratio (2026-10-08)

Source PDF: all report pdf/France_ double-digit growth in business creations, a slowdown in the rise in bankruptcies and stable non-performing loans ratio (2026-10-08).pdf
Report source: BNP Paribas
Date: 2026-10-08
Pages: 1
Status: ok

### Page 1

Paris, 8 October 2026

EcoFlash

France: double-digit growth in business creations, a slowdown in the rise in bankruptcies and 
stable non-performing loans ratio

Business creations rose by 12% y/y over the first eight months of 20261. This is significantly higher than the 5.3% y/y 
growth recorded in 2025. This increase was driven by sectors linked to information and communication technologies.

Meanwhile, business bankruptcies rose by 5.3% y/y between January and July, according to data from the Banque de 
France (compared with the 3.6% y/y increase for the whole of 2025). The rise was more pronounced in the 1st quarter (+8% y/y) 
before slowing down. This slowdown could persist, as, according to Altares, payment delays have fallen to an average of 12.3 
days in the second quarter of 2026, compared with 14.1 days a year earlier (and 13.1 days for the European average).

Rising bankruptcies are more heavily affecting the services sector, which is also experiencing slightly stronger growth 
in business creations. This correlation is particularly noticeable in the retail sector, as business creations have risen by 13% 
since the start of 2026, while bankruptcies are up by 3%. In construction and manufacturing, business bankruptcies have been 
falling since the second quarter of 2026.

The average ratio of non-performing corporate loans has remained stable at a moderately high level for the past year 
(4.0% of total loans and advances in Q2 2026). In construction, where activity is more cyclical than in other sectors, the ratio of 
non-performing loans has reached its highest level since 2019, standing at 9.5%.

The proportion of loans that have deteriorated but are not yet non-performing (“Stage 2” according to IFRS 9 terminology) 
is stable, standing at 15.4% q/q, and remains below its all-time high (16.1%). This indicator, which helps to anticipate trends in 
non-performing loans, is relatively encouraging.

Despite a significant increase since 2021, the economic weight of bankruptcies remains, on average, below its peak in 
Q1 2025 (0.53 compared with 0.61, respectively). This indicator generally allows bankruptcies to be weighted based on the size 
of the businesses. However, the economic weight of bankrupted small businesses is at a historically high level (2.11).

Take a look at our EcoCharts on trends in business creations and bankruptcies in France and the 
associated risks. It will be updated on a quarterly basis.

Thomas Humblot 
Economiste, team: Banking economics 
thomas.humblot@bnpparibas.com

Stéphane Colliac 
Head of Advanced economies, France 
stephane.colliac@bnpparibas.com

1 For more details, see Business creations and bankruptcies in France: double-digit growth in business creations, lower growth in bankruptcies, 
and stable non-performing loans, 8 October 2026.

Document 354: Global Views_ Downside Growth Risks

Source PDF: all report pdf/Global Views_ Downside Growth Risks.pdf
Report source: Goldman Sachs Publishing
Date: 2026-10-26
Pages: 10
Status: ok

### Page 1

Jan Hatzius 
+1(212)902-0394 | jan.hatzius@gs.com 
Goldman Sachs & Co. LLC
Global Views: Downside Growth Risks
26 October 2026 | 6:45AM EDT
1. So far in 2026, global growth has been remarkably unremarkable.  Both the world 
as a whole and the G3 economies are growing in line with our estimates of potential 
GDP.  Our interpretation is that fiscal easing and the positive impulse from the AI 
boom have offset the negative impulse from the war in Iran and the lingering drag 
from higher US tariffs.

2. Our standing global GDP forecasts project more of the same in 2027, as lower 
energy prices offset fading tailwinds from fiscal policy and AI.  But the balance of 
risks is on the downside.  The most immediate concern is that, far from subsiding, 
energy prices have again climbed sharply on fears of renewed Middle East escalation.  
The prices of gasoline, diesel, jet fuel, and European/Asian natural gas are now near 
or even above their March/April highs, with likely negative effects on consumers and 
energy-importing economies more broadly.

### Page 2

3. Although we don’t expect significant fiscal policy changes in 2027, the consequences 
of large-scale deficits—coupled with AI-linked borrowing and central bank hikes—have 
started to show up more clearly in higher longer-term interest rates.  The moves have 
been mostly orderly, but the increase since early 2026 averages a sizable 100bp across 
DM yield curves.  If sustained, we estimate that this increase will subtract a cumulative 
0.6% from GDP via weakness in housing, consumer durables, and business investment.  
(Only part of the drag is incorporated in our standing forecasts because we expect bond 
yields to pull back as central banks deliver fewer hikes than priced in markets.)

Exhibit 2: Oil Price Risks Are Skewed to the Upside

Source: Goldman Sachs Global Investment Research
26 October 2026   2
Goldman Sachs Global Views

### Page 3

4. The growth impulse from the AI boom was set to peak in late 2026 or early 2027 even 
before concerns around the sustainability of the AI investment boom intensified in 
recent weeks.  Although AI investment is still rising rapidly, we expect the increase as a 
share of GDP to slow over the next year.  Alongside the fading boost from last year’s 
increase in US depreciation allowances, this will weigh on capital spending growth, 
especially in the US.  AI has also boosted US consumer spending growth via a positive 
wealth effect worth an estimated 0.4pp (or 0.25% of GDP) over the past year.  This 
impact is likely to decline over the next year, gradually if stocks perform in line with our 
strategists’ reasonably upbeat expectations but more sharply if AI-related stocks 
continue to decline.

5. Our own base case remains that AI investment will ultimately pay off in terms of higher 
productivity and economywide profits.  However, one key channel for this payoff is labor 
market disruption, which has negative near-term macroeconomic effects.  And while DM 
labor markets still look solid in aggregate, we are starting to see clearer signs that the AI 
impact is spreading beyond the technology sector.  Across nonmanufacturing industries, 
the share of US firms that have adopted AI in regular business functions now shows a 
statistically significant negative correlation with employment growth and a statistically 
significant positive correlation with unemployment.

6. The Fed’s recent signals—explicit from Williams/Jefferson and implicit from Warsh—
effectively rule out a hike on October 28.  But the FOMC still looks very likely to deliver 
another 25bp hike to 4-4.25% on December 9 because spot inflation remains too high.  
And while we continue to expect significant disinflation next year on the back of 
reduced impulses from tariffs, energy prices, and AI-related prices, the risks to our 
forecast are on the upside, most obviously in energy.  Higher inflation could result in

another hike in March, but either way we see renewed cuts in late 2027/early 2028.

7. Like the Fed, the ECB will likely hold rates steady next week and deliver a final hike (to 
2.75%) at the December meeting.  However, the turbulence in the French bond market 
poses somewhat greater downside risks to that call than in the US.  The turbulence has 
tightened financial conditions to a degree that is likely to shave ¼-½pp from annualized 
growth in coming quarters.  And if it intensifies into December, markets might perceive a 
hike as signaling insufficient focus by the ECB on the risk of Euro area fragmentation.  
While the ECB could address this issue by coupling a hike with renewed balance sheet 
expansion, the hurdle for such a move is high, in part because purchases of French 
bonds would inevitably raise concerns about moral hazard with respect to the needed 
fiscal adjustment.

### Page 6

November 5.  Consistent with our view that the UK economy is stronger than widely 
perceived, GDP growth has surprised on the upside this year and the unemployment rate 
declined to 4.7% in August on a 3-month average basis (with the volatile 1-month rate 
plunging to 4.1%).  Rising inflation and the looming household energy price reset in 
January imply a significant risk of further moves, although market pricing of 4+ hikes 
(including November) still looks excessive.  We also expect hikes in Canada and New 
Zealand this week and in Australia next week.

9. Slightly faster than expected growth in Q3 has pushed up our 2026 GDP forecast for 
China to 4.6%.  But most activity indicators are much weaker.  Both property activity and 
fixed asset investment are contracting year-on-year, and while nominal retail sales 
growth picked up to +1.1% in September, large retailers (whose sales are relatively 
better measured) are still posting substantial year-on-year declines.  With domestic 
demand rising slowly at best, sustained GDP growth in the official 4.5-5% target range 
will require ongoing steep increases in the trade surplus.  But the surplus is already at 1% 
of global GDP, and further increases will eat more noticeably into output and 
employment abroad.  Ultimately, China’s trading partners may respond by imposing 
trade barriers that weigh on Chinese exports and GDP, although the measures taken so 
far have been limited.

Exhibit 8: UK Growth Forecasts Have Drifted Higher

Source: Bloomberg, Goldman Sachs Global Investment Research
26 October 2026   6
Goldman Sachs Global Views

### Page 7

10. Our strongest global view is that central banks will deliver fewer hikes than currently 
priced.  In addition, our rates strategists flag underweight positioning, diminished 
technical risks, and risk asset pressures as reasons why the recent rally might extend.  
The market’s narrow breadth makes our equity strategists wary of momentum factor 
exposure, but they still expect the S&P 500 to resume its climb after the midterm 
election, as earnings continue to grow solidly.  Our credit strategists remain cautious on 
IG technology bonds but see scope for some near-term reversal in HY given the scale of 
the recent selloff.  Our FX strategists expect the Japanese yen to act as a hedge against 
further AI jitters, in part because such jitters would shrink US-Japan interest rate 
differentials.  And our commodity strategists recommend targeted exposures to oil 
products and European natural gas to hedge near-term escalation risks alongside 
longer-term exposures to copper, gold, and select power markets to benefit from 
structural supply/demand imbalances. 
Jan Hatzius

► 美伊外交持續斡旋，卡達與巴基斯坦提出折
衷方 案 ， 一度 增 添 市場 對 兩 方談 判 突 破預
期，然雙方對核問題意見仍分歧，疊加美以
雙方準備進行軍事備戰，恐於期中選後加大
對伊朗打擊，加劇中東局勢不確定性與市場
擾動。市場持續聚焦美國企業財報季表現，
金融業財報良好開局形成股價下方支撐，而
賽默飛世爾科技等醫療企業營收、獲利與展

望皆高於預期，推動醫療類股漲勢。
► CoreWeave融資成本續升加劇AI產業擔憂，
半導體與科技類股表現顯弱。AI巨頭財報由
Meta平台打頭陣，AI驅動廣告效率與營收與
展望 優 於 預期 並 上 調全 年 資 本支 出 下 緣。
SpaceX獲利由虧轉盈且優於市場共識為股價
添柴火，Tesla財報好壞參半，短期內飛天跑
車貢獻不大，後續聚焦潛在合併所面臨的地
緣風險。通訊企業財報方面，儘管AT&T與網
飛財報優於預期，但面對衛星通訊競爭疑慮
及與第四季展望低於預期，加深通訊服務類
股下跌壓力。

► 美國財政部20年期公債拍賣結果顯示需求低
於預期，財政部標售20年期公債共130億美
元，最高得標殖利率達5.469%，為1986年1
月以來20年期公債拍賣最高利率，長天期美
債需求仍疲。信用債市面對AI大額發債與資
金回收效率擔憂，信用債利差一度擴大，隨
企業財報揭幕，市場資金流入具防禦性質的
高信評債種，如美國投資級債與亞洲投資級
債表現相對抗跌。
► 美伊衝突可能進一步擴大，美國公布截至上
周原油庫存減少310萬桶，降幅高於市場預
期，地緣風險難消與原油供需失衡壓力，油
價震盪上行。加密貨幣市場亦見反彈，美國
貨幣監理署(OCC)發布公告敲定GENIUS法
案最終實施細則，疊加近期機構投資者對加
密貨幣接受度提升推動近期漲勢。匯市主要
受到美伊戰事添通膨變數，投資人靜待下週
聯準會利率決策結果，美元相對承壓。

► 今年以來適逢美伊衝突難題遲遲未解，高油
價讓聯準會重啟升息且堆高升息預期，面對
部分聯準會官員保持觀望立場，利率期貨顯
示聯準會將升息一碼且時點可能在12月，未
來1年則存在3碼潛在升息空間，考量地緣衝
突短期 內持 續， 若 通膨難 以改善 至政 策目
標，預期聯準會2027年將維持緊縮貨幣政策
的鷹派立場來對抗通膨，然美國2年期公債利
率已多反映未來升息預期，後續仍視美國核
心物價發展，引導短天期公債利率走向。
► 儘管全球利率大幅上升，然對於整體金融環
境影響甚微，反映美股和信用市場低波動狀
態，體現實質政策利率低於緊縮利率區間與
彭博金融情勢指數變化不大，當前美國貨幣
政策與市場流動性仍偏寬鬆，倘若地緣擾動
持續造成通膨居高難下，恐進一步推升聯準

會升息預期，疊加政府財政風險與貨幣政策
透明度降，加劇長天期公債利率上行風險，
長天期債以交易為主，不建議長期持有。

► 特斯拉3Q26財報好壞參半 — 毛利率與交付
量超預 期， 營收 大 致符合 ，營業 利潤 遜預
期，最大意外是自由現金流達15億美元，高
於共識負43億，資本開支指引下調成憂慮。
► 早前公司展示下代Roadster能力，本質上是
光環車，定價高受眾少，2028年才有望投
產，盈利貢獻有限。Model 3/Y成為大眾商
品，競爭激烈，利潤率壓縮。正如傳統品牌
以賽道或參賽展示技術，「飛天」旨在展示
獨有跨界整合能力，維持品牌形象溢價。
► 至於和SpaceX的潛在合併，核心在於貫通多
頂 業 務 ， 提 高 資 本 運 用 效 率 ， 共同分擔
Terafab的開支等，現時兩家合計市值約為
3.5兆美元。這項巨型潛在交易，除了要跨過
極高表決門檻外，SpaceX具極敏感的美國國
防及NASA合作背景，而特斯拉有超過20%
業務倚重上海超級工廠，合併存在嚴重地緣
衝突。預期馬斯克將在川普離任前，加速執
行Tesla與SpaceX 合併。

► 今年9月適逢地緣擾動持續推升高油價、聯準
會重啟 升息 、財政部與企 業發債 浪潮 等影
響，形塑債市今年以來單月最差表現。檢視
2021年以來美國投資級債前十大單月最差總
報酬，2022年聯準會3月啟動升息年度就佔
一半以上，顯示利率風險仍為近5年來債市最
大風險，然檢視單月最差報酬表現未來期間
總報酬，除2022年1月和3月聯準會重啟升息
前夕與當月表現明顯較弱，多數於未來1年後
多呈現正報酬，反映隨利率走升放大孳息效
益，同時挹注債券長期持有之報酬空間。
► 今年來債券市場利率波動明顯推升信用債利
差，其 中僅 以低 信 評債利 差升幅 較顯 ，此
外，9月以來利率連動度高及AI相關產業債種
也成為利差擴大重災區，凸顯債市投資人減
輕市場不確定性的投資傾向，考量信用債市
歷經利率上行利空洗禮，信用利差同步擴大

創造收益空間，可把握債信體質仍佳企業債
鎖利良機，並透過時間複利力量強化收益。
利率波動升，僅低信評利差擴大程度相對明顯9月為美國投資級債今年最差單月報酬
用時間換空間，單月最差表現形塑未來報酬機會

### Page 8

► 受聯準會重啟升息影響，過去一年美國公債
殖利率曲線持續陡峭化，其中利率最大升幅
落在短天期的2至3年期間，美國核心通膨與
就業仍具韌性，聯準會將持續保持緊縮貨幣
政策步調來應對高通膨，考量美國高物價與
高利率環境短期難消，宜透過縮短債券配置
天期並進行動態調整，為因應後續利率不確
定性環境的良策。
► 為因應利率不確定性，投資人可採取債券梯
的投資配置，主要選取不同到期期間及不同
類型債劵進行組合，債券梯投資重點主要在
於，根據殖利率曲線型態及利率動態建立不
同期間債券梯，假設市場利率走升且殖利率
曲線正斜率，建立短天期債券梯除了降低利
率風險，有 短期 閒 置資金 及孳息 需求 投資
人，亦可取得穩定孳息收益。若後續投資人
有短期資金需求，可以視情況將短期債券到
期後資金回收，降低只投資單一債券須持至
到期或直接變現的流動性風險。

美國公債殖利率曲線持續為正斜率
高利率波動持續，用策略迎戰市場擾動

態決定後續配置

轉投資股市
短天期債券梯：假設買入2檔各3、4、5及7年到期債券，
聚焦短期穩定收益
► 債券梯是一種分散投資方式，主要選取不同到期期間及不同
類型債劵進行組合，可兼具：
1) 分散利率風險：將資金分別買進不同到期年期的債券，以分
散利率風險。
2) 分散信用風險：分散投資不同發行機構債券，不會重壓單一
債券並可分散信用違約風險。
3) 降低流動性風險：若投資人有短期資金需求，可以視情況將
短期債券到期後資金回收，降低只投資單一債券須持至到期
或直接變現之流動性風險。

### Page 9

9
股市
 美股第三季獲利年增率預估為雙位數成長，市場靜待財報結果與展望，和對AI資本支出回收影響。
考量地緣衝突、殖利率偏高、評價面壓力與鄰近美國期中選舉，短期金融市場紛擾不止，可增加防
禦股增強耐震度。長期AI、科技發展趨勢不變，逢股市拉回時可分批布局，分散配置於不同國家與
產業，降低市場整體波動，亦可以槓鈴策略(防禦+成長)穩中求利。
 歐、日股可作為分散配置重點，全球利率走揚有利銀行股。此外，地緣衝突頻傳，歐洲國防軍工仍
有投資題材。
策略風格：可增加防禦股配置，中長期看
好AI題材如科技及半導體，非AI部分關注
醫療保健生技產業
區域：日本銀行股、歐洲國防
債市
 全球公債殖利率攀升，預估今年底前主要國家仍有升息空間，殖利率偏高環境使中短天期債券提供
吸引力，逢利率走高、鎖利領息。高信評債體質較佳，具穩定現金流與還款能力，聚焦風險調整後
利差具吸引力者，包含金融、通訊及公用事業等產業。
債種：中短天期高信評債鎖利，產業如金
融、通訊及公用事業等
匯市
 歐洲政治風險上升、歐元短期弱盤，美元高檔有撐。

 日本銀行再度升息時間不定，日圓區間震盪。
美元高檔有撐
商品
 中東地緣衝突短期難解，油價維持高位但震盪加劇。
 短期美國雙率走高，使金價短期承壓，惟美歐日財政赤字壓力與去美元化，使全球央行與長期投資
人買盤依舊，提供黃金中長期穩定支撐。
短期黃金有壓，中長期維持偏多看法
類別 市場看法 偏好資產
策略思維
策略思維 防禦為上築防線，擾動休止再回成長

### Page 18

個別客戶因年齡、投資能力、金融商品交易經驗及風險承受度不同，適合之服務及/或金融商品亦有不同，應進一步與專屬理財業務人員進行評估。投資人應徹底瞭解相關服務及金融商品之風險，並審慎評估個人財力、資產狀況、
理財需求及風險承受能力，獨立判斷並自行決定是否投資及承擔有關風險。投資任何商品皆具有風險，商品以往之績效不保證未來之投資收益，本公司不負責商品之盈虧，也不保證最低之收益。
本公司保留對本文件及其內容之著作權及其他相關權利，任何人未經本公司同意，不得翻印或作其他任何用途使用(包括但不限於傳送或轉發予其他人士等)。如有違反情事，本公司除不負任何責任外，亦將對未經同意之使用與散布
等行為究責。
基金警語
投資一定有風險，基金投資有賺有賠，申購前應詳閱公開說明書
【信託產品投資須知及風險預告：（請投資人審慎評估下列各項條文內容）】
1. 基金（包括具有定期配息之基金）投資具投資風險，此一風險可能使信託本金發生全部虧損。
2. 基金委託人須自負盈虧，本公司受託投資不保本不保息，除盡善良管理人注意義務外，投資所可能產生的本金虧損、匯率損失、或基金解散、清算、移轉、合併等風險，均由委託人承擔。

3. 本公司受託投資之基金經金管會核准或同意生效，惟不表示絕無風險。基金經理公司以往之經理績效不保證基金之最低投資收益；基金經理公司除盡善良管理人之注意義務外，不負責基金之盈虧，亦不保證最低之收益，委託人
申購前應詳閱基金公開說明書。
4. 投資前應詳閱風險預告及各該基金公開說明書，確實作好財務規劃與風險評估。本公司受託投資之基金已備有公開說明書(或其中譯本)或投資人須知【前述文件併揭露有關基金應負擔之費用(境外基金含分銷費用)】，委託人可
至本公司營業處所索取或至公開資訊觀測站、基金資訊觀測站或本公司網站下載。
5. 為保護既有投資人之權益，部份境外基金設有價格稀釋調整機制及公平市價規定，投資人申購前應詳閱基金公開說明書及投資人須知。
6. 委託人瞭解並同意受託人辦理特定金錢信託業務(即委託人委託投資基金)交易時，自交易相對人取得之報酬、費用、折讓等各項利益，得作為受託人收取之信託報酬；另委託人在持有基金期間，受託人仍持續收受經理費分成報
酬，委託人可至受託人(即本公司)網站 (https://www.kgi.com.tw/zh-tw/)查詢基金之通路報酬。其基金經理費率及其分成費率與其他通路報酬最新變動情形，亦可至該網站查詢。

7. 後收型基金在贖回時，基金公司將依持有期間長短收取不同比率之遞延申購手續費，該費用將自贖回總額中扣除；另後收型基金若有收取分銷費，則申購手續費雖可遞延收取，惟每年仍需支付0%~1.06%的分銷費（按基金日平
均淨資產價值計算），可能造成實際負擔費用增加（分銷費依各基金公司而有不同，請參考本公司後收型基金投資費用暨特約事項及各基金之投資人須知），另該費用已反映於每日基金淨值中，委託人無需額外支付。
8. 委託人之交易如經基金公司認定為短線交易者，基金公司或本公司可拒絕受理申請轉換或申購要求，並可請求支付一定比例之贖回費用或其他相關費用，費用標準均依各該基金公司之規定。
9. 委託人因不同時間進場，將有不同之投資績效，過去之績效亦不代表未來績效之保證。基金配息率不代表基金報酬率，且過去配息率不代表未來配息率；基金淨值可能因市場因素而上下波動，投資人於獲配息時，宜一併注意基
金淨值之變動。又投資新興市場可能比投資已開發國家有較大的價格波動及流動性較低的風險，委託人應慎選投資標的。
10. 部分基金可能持有衍生性商品部位，該部位可能超過基金淨資產價值之100%，並可能因而造成基金淨值高度波動及衍生其他風險。

11. 基金的配息可能由基金的收益或本金中支付。任何涉及由本金支出的部分，可能導致原始投資金額減損；且部分基金配息前未先扣除應負擔之費用。由本金支付配息之相關資料，投資人可至證券投資信託事業或總代理人之公司
網站查詢。
12. 非投資等級債券基金（本類基金主要係投資於非投資等級之高風險債券且配息來源可能為本金）（主要特色係投資前揭基金之組合基金亦同），適合能承受較高風險之『非保守型』委託人且不宜占投資組合過高的比重。由於非
投資等級債券之信用評等未達投資等級或未經信用評等，且對利率變動的敏感度甚高，可能會因利率上升、市場流動性下降，或債券發行機構違約不支付本金、利息或破產而蒙受虧損，不適合無法承擔相關風險之委託人；又如
係本國投信機構募集者，則可能投資於符合美國Rule 144A規定具有私募性質之債券，雖其投資總金額不得超過基金淨資產價值之百分之三十，然該債券具有債券發行人違約之信用風險、利率風險、流動性風險、交易對手風險
及因財務訊息揭露不完整或因價格不透明導致波動性較大之風險，投資人應審慎評估；另證券投資信託事業募集之債券型基金、平衡型基金、不動產證券化基金、以追蹤、模擬或複製標的指數表現之指數股票型基金（ETF）及

指數型基金或募集之基金投資新興市場國家債券達基金淨資產價值之百分之六十以上者，或有可能投資符合美國Rule 144A規定具有私募性質之債券，亦應留意相關風險。
13. 投資於中國之基金應遵守中華民國相關法令之規定：境外基金(不含經金管會核准向證交所申請上市或交易之境外ETF)如有投資大陸地區之有價證券者，以掛牌上市有價證券及銀行間債券市場為限，且投資前述有價證券總金額
不得超過該境外基金淨資產價值之百分之二十(境外基金機構依鼓勵境外基金深耕計畫向金管會申請並經認可者得適用鼓勵境外基金深耕計畫所列放寬前揭投資總金額上限至百分之四十之優惠措施)；證券投資信託基金雖可投資
大陸地區之有價證券，惟仍應符合信託契約、公開說明書、證券投資信託基金管理辦法等相關規定及受到基金公司QFII額度之限制。又投資人須留意中國市場之政府政策、法令、會計稅務制度、經濟與市場等變動所可能產生之
投資風險。
14. 各基金於公開（含簡式）說明書或投資人須知揭露之風險報酬等級，係依據投信投顧公會「基金風險報酬等級分類標準」而訂定，本公司亦依據投信投顧公會「基金風險報酬等級分類標準」訂定基金產品風險等級。

富管理客服專線或透過財富管理客服信箱服務，查詢申訴案件處理進度。

Document 380: New Zealand 2026 General Election_ Preliminary results and next steps (2026-11-17)

### Page 1

1
Westpac New Zealand Economics
• As polls suggested, the election was a nailbiter
that has not yet been resolved. Election night
results show a lead to the centre-right on
paper in a significantly enlarged parliament.
• The centre-right bloc of National, NZ First
and ACT won 67 seats. The centre-left bloc
of Labour, Greens and Te Pāti Māori won 57
seats. There was a swing away from National
to its coalition partners while the centre left
grouping picked up a few seats.

• The Opportunity Party delivered on its polling
and will enter parliament with at least 7 seats,
reducing what could have been a larger swing
to the centre-left grouping.
• The size of parliament will expand by around 9
seats as National held onto a large share of its
electorate votes, entitling it to more seats than
implied by its party vote share. As a result,
a bloc would require 66 seats to command a
majority in parliament.
• Several senior National ministers have not

been re-elected, including Finance Minister
Nicola Willis. She will continue in a caretaker
role but will need to be replaced in any new
centre-right government.
• Significant uncertainty about the result exists.
National might lose perhaps two marginal
electorates in the final analysis. There are
400,000 special votes to count which may
shift the list seat allocations as these tend
to reduce the share of the centre-right bloc
by 1.6-1.7ppts. Hence, when all is said and

done, the centre-right’s 10 seat advantage on
election night could reduce by 1-4 seats.
• Should this play out the centre-right would not
have sufficient seats to reach a majority in an
enlarged house and would require the support
of the Opportunity Party to govern.
• The final election outcome will not be
known until 27 November. Serious coalition
negotiations likely won’t begin until then
and delays could occur. Should recounts be
required, some further delay is possible.

• Based on these results, we see a higher
probability of a centre-right government
ultimately emerging that likely will require
the support of the Opportunity Party in some
form. The Opportunity Party likely won’t enter
Cabinet but may offer support on confidence
and supply in exchange for policy concessions.
• But a centre-left government can’t be ruled
out if special votes heavily favour the centre
left bloc, and especially if an agreement
between the Opportunity Party and the centre-

right parties can’t be reached.
• Regardless of the final count, the results
suggest a government with weak support and
ongoing vulnerabilities. Hence the prospect of
controversial large changes may be modest.
For a centre-right government that might
limit the extent of fiscal consolidation, while
the centre left’s plans for larger scale tax and
spending initiatives could be trimmed back.
• A centre-right government won’t change the
RBNZ mandate while a centre-left government

likely still will. A new finance minister will be
required with the possibility of ACT leader
David Seymour taking on that role.

New Zealand 2026 General Election:
Preliminary results and next steps.

Kelly Eckhold, Chief Economist | +64 9 348 9382 | +64 21 786 758 | kelly.eckhold@westpac.co.nz

### Page 2

2
Westpac New Zealand Economics
The result.
The preliminary results of the 2026 General Election,
based on the election night count, are set out in the table
above. In summary:
• The centre-right bloc of National, NZ First and ACT
won 67 seats.
• The centre-left bloc of Labour, Greens and Te Pāti
Māori won 57 seats.
• The Opportunity Party delivered on its polling and will
enter parliament with 7 seats.
These results indicate a swing away from National to its

coalition partners while the centre-left grouping picked
up a few seats. The big gainer was the Opportunity Party
who perhaps took a big share of what otherwise could
have been a larger swing to the centre-left grouping.
The size of parliament will expand by around nine seats,
as National held onto a large share of its electorate
votes and hence has more seats than it would have been
entitled to based on its party vote share. As a result, a
governing bloc would require 66 seats to command a

majority in parliament.
Several of the electorate seats are very tight (see Table
2), so election night majorities could easily be overturned
once special votes are counted. Of particular relevance
is the seat of Wairarapa, where the National candidate is
leading the Labour candidate by just 139 votes. If Labour
were to win that seat (specials favoured Labour in 2020
but National in 2023), all else equal the centre-right seat
count would decline to 66 seats in what would become

a 130-seat Parliament – just enough to command a
majority. But if either NZ First or ACT were to lose a single
party vote seat to the left bloc once specials are counted
then that majority would disappear.
On that score, historically the election night results have
tended to overstate the support of the centre-right bloc
and understate the support for parties on the left of
the political spectrum. For example, over the previous
three elections (2017, 2020, 2023), the current centre-

right bloc’s vote share has declined 1.9%, 1.7% and 1.6%
respectively following the counting of special votes, with
the National Party losing 2 seats on each occasion once
the official results were declared (Labour, the Greens and
Te Pāti Māori have benefited). On this occasion, given its
overhang, a lower party vote share would not lower the
National Party’s final vote count, although it could alter
the size of its overhang and thus the number of seats

required to command a majority. In recent elections both
NZ First and ACT have seen their vote shares decline
following the counting of special votes.
The process from here.
Official results.
The official results will be declared on 27 November,
adding the results of an estimated 400,000 special votes
(with around a quarter of these cast overseas) to the
2.41m votes counted on election night.
We expect discussions between potential coalition
partners will begin immediately to start sketching out the

parameters of a coalition agreement. But given the tight
margins of the current vote count, no firm positions will
be agreed until the results are declared.
The writ and recounts.
After 27 November, the writ is scheduled to be returned
on 3 December – formally certifying who has been
elected. However, that date is subject to any recounts
